# WORKLOG.md — SyllabAI Chronological Work Log

## 2026-09-02 — Project pack bootstrap (external assistant)

**Task:** Establish canonical engineering context for SyllabAI.

**Completed:**
- consolidated research-paper concepts into the Master Technical Specification (v1.0);
- defined Java 25 + Spring Boot 4.1.x backend; Next.js 16 frontend on Vercel; Neon PostgreSQL + pgvector;
- defined multi-repository, modular-monolith architecture;
- added learner-model, diagnostic, Smart Mark, provenance, research, and experiment architecture;
- converted the feature inventory into a definitive project-management workbook (158 rows).

**Findings:** the full architecture is broader than a chatbot (learner model, assessment, diagnostics, KG, provenance, telemetry are equally structural); Paper B's storage proposal simplifies operationally to PostgreSQL + pgvector behind abstractions; specialist parsers belong behind contracts, not inside Java.

## 2026-09-03 — Verification, merge, and repository bootstrap

**Task:** Independently verify the v1.0 pack, merge corrections, and stand up the GitHub repositories.

**Completed:**
- verified platform claims with 7 fresh searches: Spring Boot 4.1.1 (Java 17-26) ✅, Spring Boot 3.x EOL 2026-06-30 ✅, Spring AI 2.0 GA (requires Boot 4.x) ✅, Next.js 16.3.3 Active LTS (2026-08-25 security release) ✅, Cloudflare R2 free tier no-credit-card ✅, Groq free tier ~14.4K req/day no-card ✅, **SurrealDB = BSL 1.1, NOT Apache-2.0 ❌ (v1.0 dossier error)**;
- merged pack → v1.1: free-LLM chain added (Groq → Gemini 2.5 Flash → OpenRouter, ADR-009); Cycle-1 pilot scope locked (ADR-010, Master Spec §39a); polyglot policy (ADR-011); 4 repos (ADR-012); license wall (ADR-013); SurrealDB struck everywhere;
- merged backlog → v2: 160 rows; ADDED F-159 decay job, F-160 learning-log telemetry, F-161 Smart Mark κ gate, F-162 timed-vs-untimed fluency gap, F-163 free-LLM chain; PATCHED 10 legacy rows (F-001 AWS→Render/Vercel/Neon, F-008 Celery→Spring jobs, F-020/F-032/F-033/F-034/F-039/F-040/F-122 stack-contradiction fixes, F-047 κ metric); removed blank rows F-110/112/114; added `Cycle` column (34 Cycle-1 rows, 12-spine);
- integrated 38-repo Java-fit analysis into `REPOSITORY_RESEARCH.md` section 0 (verdicts: USE-DIRECT opendataloader-pdf/os-taxonomy/Scientific-learning-skills/pdfcn/A11Y.md; OFFLINE mineru/surya/anydoc/pdf-inspector; REFERENCE DeepTutor/get-it/Understand-Anything/open-notebook; WATCH zvec/helix-db/pgcontext; STRUCK SurrealDB);
- created GitHub repositories (private): `syllabai`, `syllabai-core`, `syllabai-web`, `syllabai-parser`; pushed this pack to `syllabai` (main repo).

**Mistakes / regressions:**
- Mistake (inherited from v1.0, corrected): SurrealDB recommended as Tier-A "Apache-2.0" backend — verified BSL 1.1 and struck (ADR-013). Any future doc claiming permissive licensing of an external repo must cite the license text, not the dossier.
- Mistake (caught pre-merge): v1.0 backlog contradicted its own ADRs (Neo4j rows vs ADR-005 Postgres graph; Celery row vs ADR-001 Java; Pinecone row vs ADR-004). All fixed and marked `PATCHED v2`.

**Breakthroughs / useful insights:**
- opendataloader-pdf is Java + Apache-2.0 on Maven Central — runs in-process inside `syllabai-core`; no separate parsing service for text PDFs; bounding boxes feed the citation chain.
- Pilot LLM budget fits Groq free tier: 50 students × ~20 queries/day ≈ 1K req/day vs ~14.4K/day capacity.
- Nothing in the 38-repo set implements BKT/BDT/IRT/telemetry/Smart Mark — the scientific core is greenfield Java, which is exactly the course project's showcase.

**Open questions:**
- Exact Edexcel IAL Chemistry content licensing posture for a private pilot (fine; re-check before any public flip).
- Gemini 2.5 Flash free-tier stability at build time (re-verify).
- κ-gate marker capacity for double-marking (recruiting task, Wave 4).

**Next action:** TODO T-001 — bootstrap `syllabai-core` (Spring Boot 4.1 + Java 25 + Spring AI 2.0 skeleton).

## 2026-09-03 — Build session: Wave 0 + science core (T-001…T-018, T-020, T-023)

**Repos / commits:**

- `syllabai-core` @ `71f873d` — T-001…T-007 + T-012/T-014…T-018/T-020/T-023 (see core README "Implemented so far").
- `syllabai-web` @ `3c410ed` — T-005 Learner Workbench (login, practice, mastery map, my state).

**What landed:**

- Java 25 + Spring Boot 4.1.1 + Spring AI 2.0.1 modular monolith; 13-module package map per ADR-012.
- Flyway V1–V7: identity (roles/users/user_roles), curriculum + KG nodes/edges, question bank + attempts, learner states, research telemetry/registries, seed (Edexcel IAL Chemistry WCH11: 3 topics × 2 subtopics, 4 documented misconceptions, 8 MCQs with distractor→misconception tags, model registry v1-cycle1 rows).
- JWT auth + RBAC; springdoc OpenAPI; global error handling; GH Actions CI (JDK 25).
- Science: BKT engine (paper params, config + model_versions registry), BDT engine (prior 0.3), Ebbinghaus decay (τ 30/90/365 by band, floor, review threshold) + nightly job; evidence contract as domain events (Observer): attempts → `AssessmentEvidenceRecordedEvent` → learner model + telemetry.
- KG: recursive-CTE prerequisite closure, subtree, misconceptions; tree/prereq/misconception endpoints.
- LLM: `LlmProvider` port + Spring AI adapters + `FailoverLlmChain` (Groq → Gemini → OpenRouter, health/cooldown/failover, admin health endpoint) — app boots with zero keys.
- Storage: `ObjectStorage` port + local + R2 (S3 SDK) adapters.
- Web: single-page workbench, typed API client, mobile-first responsive, a11y, CI (lint + type-checked build).

**Mistakes / regressions (caught and fixed):**

- MISCONCEPTION_OF edge direction inverted in `KnowledgeEdgeRepository` query → misconceptions silently absent from trees; fixed + re-verified.
- Spring AI autoconfig requires keys at boot → excluded all model autoconfigs; manual provider construction.
- Unauthenticated requests returned 403 via the /error dispatch → permitted `/error` + explicit 401 entry point.
- BKT/BDT unit-test expected values initially wrong (my arithmetic, not the engine) — corrected against hand-computed posteriors.
- Web `apiPath()` double-`?` when the path already carried a query (tree 404) — fixed with `&`-aware separator.

**Verification:**

- `mvn verify`: 32/32 unit tests green.
- Live end-to-end smoke (portable Postgres 17.11 + `local` profile): login → tree → prerequisites (depth-3 chain) → wrong answer (BKT 0.1131, BDT 0.75 exact) → correct answer (0.3832) → telemetry rows; RBAC 401/403; OpenAPI reachable; browser-verified via agent-browser (login, quiz submit, misconception alert, mastery map, state views, mobile viewport).

**Next action:** Wave 1 — T-008/T-009 canonical format + opendataloader-pdf; T-010/T-011 ingestion; T-013 pgvector; then T-019/T-021/T-022.

## 2026-09-03 — Build session 2: code audit + fixes 1–3

**Task:** Independent code-level audit of the five repositories (external audit cross-checked line-by-line), then implement the three highest-risk audit findings in `syllabai-core`.

**Audit verdict (all 19 external findings verified true; 5 highest-risk):**
1. BDT `updateOnCorrect` was provably dead code — `misconceptionIds` assembled only from the *chosen* option, and correct options are never tagged (V7 seed).
2. `FailoverLlmChain.pinnedProvider()` returned `null` — experiment pinning claimed but not implemented (schema + config existed, nobody consulted them).
3. Telemetry emitted only 2 of the 6 event types declared in the V5 schema.
4. (lower priority, deferred) `SHORT_ANSWER` declared in schema but MCQ-only submission API.
5. (docs, deferred) Master Spec says 4 repositories; `Past-Papers` (public, 689 MB, IAL/IGCSE) makes 5.

**What landed (`syllabai-core` @ `508d95d`):**

- **Fix 1 — BDT evidence assembly:** `AssessmentEvidenceRecordedEvent` now carries `observedMisconceptionIds` (every misconception the item's distractors monitor) alongside the expressed list. `LearnerModelService`: correct answer weakens all monitored misconceptions (`updateOnCorrect`), tagged wrong answer strengthens the expressed one, untagged wrong stays neutral.
- **Fix 2 — experiment pinning, fail-loud:** new `ExperimentPinResolver` port; `PropertiesExperimentPinResolver` (`syllabai.llm.experiment-pins`, `"provider"` or `"provider:model"`) + `JpaExperimentPinResolver` (V5 `experiments` registry, `RUNNING` only, read-only entity) composed config-first. Unpinned experiment ids throw with a self-documenting message; pinned experiments never fail over; pinned model flows through a new `LlmRequest.model` field into Spring AI runtime options.
- **Fix 3 — full telemetry stream:** four new domain events (`MasteryUpdated`, `MisconceptionUpdated`, `DecayApplied`, `ReviewScheduled`) published by the learner model and the nightly decay job; `TelemetryService` persists them as `BKT_UPDATED` / `BDT_UPDATED` / `DECAY_APPLIED` / `REVIEW_SCHEDULED`. All six V5 event types now flow.
- Side-fix discovered while wiring: the decay job evaluated the review threshold by re-decaying the already-decayed stored value (double-decay) — now compares the effective mastery directly.

**Verification:**

- `mvn test`: **60/60 green** (was 32): +14 chain/pinning, +4 pin parsing, +6 learner model BDT wiring, +2 evidence assembly, +6 telemetry coverage, +3 decay-job events.
- Live end-to-end on Postgres 17 (portable, `local` profile): login → Q1 wrong (BDT 0.3→0.75) → Q1 wrong again (0.75→0.9545) → **Q2 correct weakened the monitored misconception 0.9545→0.875** (hand-checked exact) with `BDT_UPDATED` evidence `CORRECT_ANSWER`, `BKT_UPDATED` mastery 0.1131→0.3869; app boots with the `experiments` entity validated by Hibernate (`ddl-auto: validate`).

**Known follow-ups (audit items 4–5, deliberately deferred):** short-answer submission strategy, 5-repo topology doc refresh (Master Spec §3 + AGENT), T-016 wording, seeder password log line, localStorage→httpOnly cookie hardening.

**Next action:** Wave 1 — T-008/T-009 canonical document format + opendataloader-pdf; T-010/T-011 syllabus/past-paper ingestion; T-013 pgvector embeddings.

## 2026-09-03 — Build session 3: content/assessment fabric (T-008–T-011, T-019, T-021, T-022 + audit fix 4)

**Task:** Execute the next build phase with a content/assessment-first strategy (no tutor UI, no broad feature expansion): multi-part assessment model, Smart Mark pipeline with the κ agreement gate, timed/untimed conditions, ingestion bridge, Testcontainers CI, structured player.

**Repos / commits:**

- `syllabai-core` @ `b745bf4` (audit fix 4) → `003b6c0` (V8–V10 + services + 91/91 tests) → `1803988` (Testcontainers IT + CI).
- `syllabai-parser` @ `a43ad24` — T-008/T-009 recovered from the interrupted session and pushed (was local-only).
- `syllabai-web` @ `3b1b5cd` — structured question player + fluency-gap Δ.
- `syllabai` — this update (5-repo topology, T-016 wording, status).

**What landed:**

- **Audit fix 4 (in-flight when the previous session died):** experiment-pin model precedence — a caller-supplied model can no longer override a registered experiment's pinned model (§26.1 precedence: pin > caller model > provider default); `LlmResponse` now reports the model actually used so telemetry never misattributes pinned requests. 61/61 at the time.
- **V8 multi-part assessment model:** exam_papers, question_versions (immutable snapshots, SUGGESTED/VALIDATED lifecycle), question_parts, mark_schemes/mark_points (decomposition + teacher-authored acceptance criteria, JSONB), answers (per-part PENDING → SMART_MARKED → HUMAN_MARKED/OVERRIDDEN), smart_mark_results + human_marks (append-only), smart_mark_agreement_evaluations, attempts marking lifecycle + `evidence_emitted` single-fire guard, skill_states.procedural_fluency_gap. Existing MCQ flow unchanged; V7 seed MCQs backfilled as VALIDATED v1.
- **Smart Mark pipeline (Strategy + validators):** `MarkingCandidateGenerator` port (LLM adapter: pinned prompt v1, temp 0.1, strict-JSON parsing, temperature provenance) → deterministic validators (bounds: no invented/duplicate point ids; coverage: every in-scope point decided; mark-sum: awards ≤ scheme bound) → append-only `SmartMarkResult` with per-point breakdown (evidence + rationale) and failure codes. Blank answers short-circuit to a deterministic zero-mark decision — no LLM call, no hallucination surface.
- **κ agreement gate (F-161):** Cohen's κ over paired per-mark-point binary decisions (latest accepted smart run × latest human mark); degenerate-marginals convention documented (κ = 1 when pe = 1 and agreement perfect); threshold 0.60 recorded per evaluation row; **fail-closed** — no evaluation rows = gated. Pre-gate smart marks are provisional (never fire evidence); post-gate smart marks are authoritative; human overrides revise marks for research and never re-fire BKT.
- **Structured submission (audit item 4 closed):** POST /api/v1/attempts/structured — one answer per part of the current version, Paper B §3.5 fields, timed/untimed tag. Evidence fires exactly once at first authoritative marking; documented conservative correctness rule for partial credit (full marks = mastery evidence; raw marks ride in the event).
- **T-011 ingestion bridge:** parser's past-paper-draft.json (schema 1.0) → POST /api/v1/teacher/content/past-papers → exam paper + STRUCTURED questions + v1 versions + parts + scheme + points, all SUGGESTED, single transaction, idempotent (409 on paper+session re-ingest). Ingestion-anchor KG topic per paper (the pipeline never guesses curriculum placement); teachers remap during review. Teacher review workflow: review queue, validate/reject paper (all-versions-first guard), version, scheme (with atomic acceptance-criteria authoring).
- **Fluency gap (T-019, F-162):** skill_states.procedural_fluency_gap = untimed accuracy − timed accuracy over graded attempts per node, recomputed on evidence, null until both conditions observed. Exposed in /learners/me/state.
- **ServableQuestionSpec (Specification pattern):** unvalidated structured content never serves — the student API filters by active + current-version VALIDATED.
- **Testcontainers IT (CI):** `MultipartMarkingFlowIT` walks the whole loop on pgvector/pgvector:pg17 via @ServiceConnection; failsafe plugin; skipped locally without Docker (this sandbox), runs in CI. TC 2.0.5 coordinates (artifacts renamed testcontainers-junit-jupiter / testcontainers-postgresql).
- **Web:** structured player (per-part textareas, submit-for-marking, pending-marks panel), Δ fluency gap in My-state. Practice submit guard bug fixed (structured path blocked by the MCQ `chosen` guard).

**Mistakes / regressions (caught and fixed):**

- LazyInitializationExceptions on detached DTO access (open-in-view is intentionally false): fixed with the repo's established pattern — EntityGraphs on the lazy collections used by controllers + read-only FK mirror columns (question_id / question_version_id) so detached views read FKs without touching proxies. First attempt preferred the proxy (non-null even when detached) — corrected to prefer the mirror.
- Scheme validation NPE on `{}` body (missing @Valid + missing null-guard) — both added.
- `Map.copyOf` in TelemetryEvent rejects null payload values → NPE for null modelId/failureReason; handlers now omit null entries.
- My κ hand-computation in a test was wrong (expected −0.4; correct 0.0 for (1,1),(0,1) with human marginal 1.0) — fixed against the formula.
- Test pitfalls: entities without ids outside JPA (TestIds reflection helper), a mock EvidencePublisher that never flips the single-fire guard (switched to the real publisher in tests), event-index assumption in the override test (select last, not get(1)).
- Testcontainers 2.x artifact rename broke the pom (junit-jupiter → testcontainers-junit-jupiter) — BOM 2.0.5 + renamed coordinates.
- Session recovery: the previous session died mid-fix with the pin-precedence diff uncommitted and T-008/T-009 committed only locally — both recovered, tested, pushed. The portable Postgres data dir had been corrupted by the tmp cleaner (pg_notify missing) — re-initialized (dev data only, re-seeded via Flyway).
- A wedged backend instance (stuck OPTIONS/health requests after heavy e2e + a logback ThrowableProxy ClassNotFound in error dispatch) — resolved by restart; the fresh instance serves identical traffic correctly. Root not fully diagnosed; watch for recurrence.

**Verification:**

- `mvn verify`: **91/91 unit tests** (was 61; +30 κ hand-computed, pipeline validators, authority/gate, human-mark evidence + override, ingestion, structured submit, telemetry marking, servable spec) + IT correctly skipped without Docker.
- Live end-to-end (portable Postgres 17.11, `local` profile): real 4CH0/1C Jan 2012 parser draft ingested (27 questions / 62 parts / 33 mark points, all SUGGESTED; duplicate ingestion → 409) → student sees 0 structured questions pre-validation (8 MCQs only) → teacher validates q7's version + scheme → paper validation correctly blocked (409, 26 versions still SUGGESTED) → student sees q7 with 3 parts → timed structured submit (blank part) → smart-mark the blank answer (deterministic, no LLM: accepted, 0 marks, provisional — no evidence) → human mark (0 marks, per-point decisions) → evidence fired exactly once, BKT 0.1151 after 2 attempts, fluencyGap computed → κ evaluation (sample=1, κ=1.00, gate PASSED) → second blank-answer smart mark became AUTHORITATIVE (evidence fired without human) → telemetry shows SMART_MARK_COMPLETED + HUMAN_MARK_RECORDED.
- Browser (agent-browser): login → 8 MCQ answers (BKT live-updating in My-state) → structured q7 player (3 part textareas) → submit for marking (201, pending-marks panel) → My-state renders Δ on the paired anchor node.

**Known follow-ups:**

- T-013 pgvector embeddings — next session (prerequisite for KA-RAG).
- Real Smart Mark LLM runs need free-tier keys (Groq/Gemini; ADR-009); no-key path fails honestly (PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE) by design.
- Parser v0 draft quality: non-unique externalRefs, many unsplit stems, marks=0 parts (confidence ~0.55) — all SUGGESTED by design; the validation workflow is the mitigation. Parser refinement + IAL Chemistry syllabus ingestion (T-010 core side) pending.
- localStorage JWT → httpOnly cookie hardening still deferred (Wave 4).
- Teacher marking/content UI is API-only (the web workbench is learner-facing); build when Wave 4 starts.
- Webhook drift: MASTER_SPEC 5-repo topology now fixed; ADR-012 amendment noted inline.

**Next action:** T-013 pgvector embedding pipeline (Gemini embeddings, mark schemes + notes) → then T-024 KA-RAG orchestration.

**Session 3 postscript — CI hardening (4 iterations, all landed green):** the first Testcontainers CI run failed and each failure was a genuine find: (1) ingestion-anchor KG code could exceed `knowledge_nodes.code` VARCHAR(40) with long paper codes — deterministic 31+8-hex-hash cap; (2) exam-paper VARCHAR fields fed from untrusted parser drafts (paper_code etc.) — all bounded; (3) IT assertions read stale in-memory entities after each service call commits its own transaction — re-fetch pattern (unit tests never see this because they share instances); (4) **real product bug: `attempts.correct` stayed the placeholder false for structured attempts after marks landed** — the fluency-gap native query reads the raw column, so every structured attempt would have zeroed its contribution to the gap. `recordTotalMarks(total, marksTotal)` now settles correctness with the same documented full-marks rule as the evidence event. Web CI fixed too (bun setup order + corrupted `branches: ain]` → `[main]` in both repos). Core @ `5b626ca`: 91/91 unit + full IT green in CI; web @ `0c09b52`: green.

## Session 12 — T-C03 pre-merge code review, regression-proof hardening, both merges, post-merge verification

- Mandated workflow followed in order: read AGENT.md, MASTER_SPEC (§6.4/§9/§10, §27 unchanged by T-C03), TODO/PROGRESS/WORKLOG, `docs/t-c02-bridge.md`, `docs/t-c03-batch.md`, parser `docs/glm-ocr/adapter-design-reconciliation.md` before touching anything.
- **Critical check A — transaction atomicity (real code trace, not the PR description):** `GlmOcrBatchService.runBatch` is `@Transactional`; it calls `GlmOcrIngestionService.ingestPair` (`@Transactional`) which calls `ContentIngestionService.ingest` and `PastPaperIngestionService.ingest` (both `@Transactional`) and the repositories. Exhaustive grep over `src/main` for `REQUIRES_NEW`, `Propagation.`, `TransactionTemplate`, `@Async`/`TaskExecutor`/`ExecutorService`/`CompletableFuture`, `TransactionStatus`/`getTransaction`: ZERO hits — no nested/suspended transactions, no async persistence, no separate transaction templates, no proxy bypass (CLI and controller both inject the proxied `GlmOcrBatchService` bean). All propagation is default REQUIRED ⇒ one physical transaction per batch. Structurally sound — but no test PROVED it.
- **Critical check B — learner-serving quarantine (the real path):** serving path = `QuestionController.list/get` → `project()` → `ServableQuestionSpec.isSatisfiedBy` — non-STRUCTURED questions serve when active; STRUCTURED questions require the current version to be VALIDATED. `PastPaperIngestionService` creates EVERY imported question as `Question.Type.STRUCTURED` with `QuestionVersion.ValidationState.SUGGESTED`, so the validation state genuinely is the authorization boundary for imports. Also checked: `ExamPaperController` (paper metadata only, validation state visible per question — never serves content), `AssessmentService.submit` (MCQ submit requires an option — imported STRUCTURED questions have none ⇒ NotFoundException), `GraphKnowledgeRetriever` (VALIDATED nodes only — mirrors the spec). The T-C02-era `GlmOcrBridgeIT.unvalidatedContentNeverServes` already exercised the real controller per-pair, but the T-C03 batch's `not-learner-servable` invariant alone was a state-field count.
- **Audit artifact cross-verification:** downloaded the CI workflow artifact of run 33983936464 (`4b820cc`) — byte-identical to the workspace copy; numbers match the issue #4 record (June 20/20/20/51 OK 80/80 findings 25; October 20/26/20/53 OK 80/null findings 27 — Q18 conflict asserted in GlmOcrBridgeIT/GlmOcrDraftMapperTest and stored verbatim in the qp_draft JSONB; 1A 19/25/19/69 REVIEW_REQUIRED 80-vs-120 preserved; row deltas +6 documents/+91 chunks/+59 versions/+59 schemes/+173 points/+3 papers/+3 bridge records; 91/91 chunks pending; idempotency pass zero new rows; all five invariants PASS). CI logs of runs 33983936464 (core) and 33983496464 (parser) downloaded and inspected: 196/196 + 20/20 IT; 71/71 + FULL CONFORMANCE — both SUCCESS, on the exact PR heads.
- **Parser PR #1 review:** diff adds exactly `GlmOcrPairCli` + `GlmOcrPairCliTest` — engine/extractors/reconciliation/canonical identity untouched; CLI composes the existing verified components; ids deterministic by construction (`CanonicalIdentity`); the extractors set `paper.canonicalDocumentId` from the parsed canonical `documentId` (structural, not re-computed — the exact invariant core's `validateBundle` checks); `--uri-prefix` produces the Past-Papers repository-relative URI shape; malformed input fails safely (existence checks, parse exceptions propagate, usage + exit 2 on wrong arity); rerun determinism asserted at byte level (drafts + reconciliation) with only `extractedAt` honestly varying. No issues found.
- **The fix the review demanded (tests only, no product code):** two regression ITs added to `GlmOcrBatchIT` (`1976540`):
  1. `lateFailureRollsBackTheWholeBatch` — builds a 4-pair batch (3 real + `zzz-poisoned-duplicate-paper`); the poison is a COPY of the 1A bundle re-identified (fresh `documentId` + `source.checksum`, patched draft claims, reconciliation untouched) so it passes bundle validation, INGESTS its two canonical documents fresh (real rows written), and only then fails at T-011's duplicate-paper refusal (`WPH11/01A` + `October 2025` = pair 3 in the same transaction). Expected and observed: `ConflictException("… already ingested")` and the ENTIRE batch rolls back to the pristine V7 seed (0 documents/chunks/papers/schemes/points/bridge records; 8 seed versions). This proves all-pairs-or-nothing INCLUDING the failing pair's own partial writes.
  2. `importedBatchContentNeverServesToLearners` — through the REAL `QuestionController`: the learner selection contains none of the 59 imported question ids; the topic-scoped query on an imported paper's anchor topic returns empty; direct fetch of an imported question 404s; POSITIVE CONTROLS: the 8 seed MCQs still appear in the selection and an authoritative question still serves by direct fetch (the gate blocks unvalidated imports, not the endpoint).
  - Test-order renumbering (rollback first on the fresh DB), autowired the six repositories + `QuestionController`; poison consistency pre-verified offline (`scripts/tc03/poison_consistency_check.py` — bundle invariants, fresh identities against V11 unique indexes, the T-011 trigger, non-empty questions); local `mvn test` 196/196 (JDK 25.0.4.1 + Maven 3.9.9, deps cached; ITs are CI-only — no Docker in sandbox, as designed).
- **Branch CI at the new head:** run **34015269882** on `1976540` — 196/196 unit + **22/22 IT (GlmOcrBatchIT 5/5)**, BUILD SUCCESS (logs inspected).

Fix (pre-merge, per the audit's own instruction): `TeacherRouteSecurityIT` — `@SpringBootTest(RANDOM_PORT)` + JDK HttpClient (TestRestTemplate no longer exists in Boot 4.1): STUDENT token proven VALID on /api/v1/learners/me/state (control — the 403s cannot pass vacuously), then 403 on all seven teacher endpoints (roster, queue, detail, smart-mark, human-mark, κ latest, κ evaluate); anonymous 401 + no email leakage; forged/wrongly-signed token 401; TEACHER 200 (roster contains the registered learner) + ADMIN 200. Local: compiles, skips without Docker (3 skipped, as designed). CI run 34093970676 on `c7d22b9`: SUCCESS, TeacherRouteSecurityIT 3/3, 240/240 total.
- Roster isolation (section 5): single-cohort pilot interpretation VERIFIED against spec (no class entity in Cycle 1; the class list IS the enabled STUDENT population — documented in the controller javadoc + the UI's "classes are a Cycle-2+ concept" framing). Enabled-only (disabled users excluded by JPQL), role filter excludes teachers/admins (IT asserts the provisioned teacher is absent), projection is exactly 4 fields and a test pins the record-component count so password hash/roles cannot leak through the API boundary.
- Marking isolation (section 6): teacher role is the route boundary (now regression-proven); any teacher marking any learner's answer is the intended single-cohort semantics (no per-teacher class ownership exists — matches spec); part bounds re-validated server-side (ConflictException, not just the client's 0..partMarks check); unknown answer/state 404 fail-loud; human-mark first/override state machine + evidence-once semantics enforced in `TeacherMarkingService` (unchanged from T-022, test-covered by TeacherMarkingServiceTest 5/5).
- κ-gate semantics (section 7): lifecycle re-traced on main — Smart Mark results are append-only and provisional; `SmartMarkService.markAnswer` lets accepted marks drive evidence ONLY when `kappaGatePassed` (newest ALL/paper evaluation κ ≥ 0.60, fail-closed on absence); human marks are authoritative; overrides revise but never re-fire evidence; `KappaAgreementService.cohenKappa` is pure, binary per-point pairing, degenerate-case convention documented. The web UI mirrors this honestly: "Latest Smart Mark (provisional)" vs "Latest human mark (authoritative)", κ panel with fail-closed copy, honest 404 "no evaluation recorded yet" / 409 "no paired decisions available yet" states, per-point decisions only offered when a smart breakdown exists. No κ semantics were altered by the PR.
- T-C04 quarantine (sections 8–9): the PR touches no GLM-OCR/ingestion/serving code (diff-limited audit, verified file-by-file); GlmOcrBatchIT 5/5 and GlmOcrBridgeIT 7/7 green in the PR run, the audit-fix run, AND both post-merge runs; no batch executed, no status changed, corpus remains exactly 3 pairs; T-C04 acceptance criteria untouched and the TODO entry remains Deferred/Ready-to-Resume with its 9 prerequisites. Route per Spec §22: `GET /api/v1/learners/me/recommendations?rootId=` (`LearnerRecommendationController`, subject-scoped like /knowledge-graph). `RecommendationProperties` (`syllabai.recommendation`) — documented v0 product tuning parameters, overridable per environment (not scientific constants). **`ServableQuestionService` extracted from `QuestionController` as the single owner of the learner-serving boundary** (active + MCQ, or STRUCTURED with a VALIDATED current version; `countServableByTopic`/`isServable`/`findById`/`activeByTopic`); the controller delegates — mechanical, behavior-identical, guarded by the existing servability regression ITs. No new persistence, no new learner-model semantics, no parallel tracking system (the T-028 read-model composition pattern; ANSWER evidence via the existing `AnswerRepository` learner scan).

- Findings: (1) a mock that violates a repository's ordering contract exposed a real robustness gap — the "overdue first" rule now sorts in the service so the rule is self-contained (defensive determinism beats trusting the contract). (2) The test helper initially undercounted SkillState attempts (the entity constructor does not set `attempts` — only `recordAttempt` increments it), which made a "below the evidence floor" fixture actually 0 attempts; fixed by counting up from zero — a reminder that JPA default-field semantics bite exactly at test boundaries. (3) `submitStructured` does not re-check validation state (only the SERVING path does) — that's existing designed behavior, and it is exactly what made the unvalidated-retry boundary IT provable: evidence can exist on now-unservable questions, and the NBA must and does exclude them via `ServableQuestionService.isServable`. (4) One scope note kept honest: problem-question targeting uses the question's PRIMARY topic; secondary question_topics mappings are a documented v1 limitation.
- Mistakes avoided: the first service draft trusted the repository's dueAt ordering (now sorted defensively); a broken char literal (`replace('_', ' ")`) was caught by the compiler before any test ran.
- Open questions / next actions: (1) the two human merge decisions in flight — core PR #8 → web PR #4 (T-033) and core PR #1 (T-026/T-027); (2) after T-033 merges: pilot-time browser verification of the full loop (dashboard actions → practice → evidence → refreshed actions) against the live backend; (3) recommendation exposure telemetry (RecommendationExposure with policy/version) deliberately deferred in v1 — the policy label ships in the response, and the evaluation layer stays a documented Cycle-2+ follow-up; (4) Wave 4 (T-030/T-031/T-032) still awaits an explicit go; T-C04 still deferred/resumable.

## Session 22 — T-036 deployment rehearsal at production fidelity: 2 deployment-blocker bugs found/fixed/PR'd, full browser E2E re-proven, operator package delivered

**Date:** 2026-09-08 · **Mode:** deploy → verify → fix → harden (T-036)

### Environment ground truth (established first, honestly)

- Re-verified remote state via GitHub API before any work: core main `c4af544`, web main `e4a06cb`, docs `053f6a6`, parser `ba36ceb` — no drift since session 21; zero open PRs.
- **No Render/Vercel/Neon/Cloudflare credentials exist in this workspace** (checked env vars, CLI tools, config dirs — nothing; the sandbox `DATABASE_URL` is an unrelated local SQLite). Actual cloud deployment is therefore **REQUIRES OPERATOR ACTION** — never fabricated. Everything short of that was executed and verified.

### Deployment rehearsal (the cloud deploy, minus the clouds)

- Toolchain rebuilt from scratch: Temurin JDK 25.0.4.1 (matches the Dockerfile's temurin-25 family), Maven 3.9.16 (Dockerfile pins 3.9.9 — image-tag vs dlcdn mirror; identical artifact semantics), portable Postgres 17.11 + pgvector 0.8.0 (distro debs extracted rootless — pgvector parity with Neon), listening on 127.0.0.1:5433.
- **Dockerfile-parity build:** `mvn -B package` with JDK 25 (the Dockerfile's exact `mvn package -DskipTests` shape, then with tests) → `syllabai-core-0.1.0-SNAPSHOT.jar` (sha `b9e7cf77…`).
- **Prod-profile boot with render.yaml's exact env shape** (`SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE=prod`, `SYLLABAI_STORAGE_TYPE=r2`, no R2/LLM/embedding secrets, runtime-generated JWT secret): Flyway V1–V14 **all success on a fresh DB in 0.17 s**, health `{"status":"UP"}` in 16 s.

### Bug 1 (deployment blocker) — found, fixed, regression-tested, PR'd

- **Reproduced:** first prod-profile boot **failed** — `Error creating bean 'objectStorage' … Access key ID cannot be blank`. render.yaml hardcodes `SYLLABAI_STORAGE_TYPE=r2` unconditionally while the R2 secrets are `sync: false` optional; every operator deploying per the runbook without pre-configured R2 gets a boot-failing deployment. DEPLOYMENT.md had no R2 section at all.
- **Fix (smallest correct layer — the storage bean):** `R2ObjectStorage` builds its S3Client **lazily on first use**; blank credentials → boot-time WARN naming the missing env vars; first storage op → `StorageException` with the exact setting names; **no silent fallback** to local/ephemeral disk (that would write production objects to Render's ephemeral filesystem). `render.yaml` comment updated; DEPLOYMENT.md §1 gains the R2-optional provisioning note.
- **Regression:** `R2ObjectStorageTest` (boot-safe construction with render.yaml's exact shape; loud per-op failure message; `isConfigured` matrix) — unit suite **273/273** (270 + 3 new).
- **PR #12** `codex/t-036-r2-boot-fix` @ `8e1f093` — CI 34243546661 SUCCESS (full 273 + 30 IT on GitHub runners). **Open, awaiting operator merge.**
- **Re-verified live post-fix:** the same env shape now boots (WARN + UP) and the 19-check backend suite passes against it.

### Bug 2 (production UX/honesty) — found, fixed, rebuilt, PR'd

- **Reproduced** in the production web build: the login screen unconditionally rendered "Demo accounts (local profile)" credentials — those accounts exist **only** under the local profile, so the deployed product advertised non-working credentials on its first screen.
- **Fix:** NODE_ENV-gated rendering (dev keeps the convenience; production build hides the box and the "demo credentials below" description). Production bundle rebuilt, browser re-verified: "Use your SyllabAI account."
- **PR #7** `codex/t-036-login-demo-creds-prod` @ `376f4c5` — CI 34244900527 SUCCESS (lint + type-checked build). **Open, awaiting operator merge.**

### Production-mode browser E2E (web standalone build → prod-profile API → external PG)

- Full student loop: register → workspace → honest empty states → practice (moles MCQ) → **deliberately wrong (misconception-matching distractor A)** → honest marking ("Not correct — 0/1", correct answer revealed, misconception signal explained) → **My state** (BKT 11%, misconception "Moles and grams are interchangeable" active 0.75) → **Dashboard NBA changed** (uncovered-starter → MISCONCEPTION_SUSPECTED ranked 1 with "Ask Tutor" deep link) → tutor deep link → honest deterministic refusal ("answering would mean guessing — which SyllabAI never does"; "Deterministic refusal — no model was called") → **retry with the correct answer under timed conditions** → BKT **0.113→0.383** (identical to session 20 — deterministic engines), BDT 0.75→0.50, `proceduralFluencyGap` **−1.0** (untimed/timed pair) → **NBA changed again** (+ fluency-gap "Timed exercise" action) → **History** (both attempts: marks, timing, source refs, marking state, misconception annotation) → **Mastery map** (curriculum tree + learner overlay; "not practised" ≠ mastery-0, misconception annotation on-node).
- §10 chain proven at each transition: browser action → network request (CORS preflight + call) → backend response → **persisted rows** (attempts/skill_states/misconception_states verified by direct SQL) → UI state.
- **Isolation:** student B registered — empty state everywhere, zero rows in DB; student→teacher/admin routes 403 (API-verified + regression ITs).
- **Teacher E2E:** teacher provisioned via the runbook's documented one-off local-profile procedure; roster (6 real learners), marking queue (honest empty across 4 states), **κ fails closed** (409 "needs paired per-point decisions"). Honest scope finding: **no seeded structured questions exist** — the Smart Mark/human-mark/κ cycle needs T-C04-validated content first; teacher KG lens is Cycle-2+ by tracker design (F-035/TFA-03).
- **Error states:** invalid login → verbatim "invalid credentials"; corrupt-token reload → session cleared + "Dashboard unavailable — try signing in again" (no fabricated data); backend unreachable (routes aborted) → "Cannot reach the SyllabAI backend. Is it running?"

### Security pass (source + empirical)

- SecurityConfig (stateless JWT, route RBAC, exact-origin CORS), JwtService (≥32-byte fail-fast, HS256, 12 h), JwtAuthenticationFilter (no token logging), GlobalExceptionHandler (opaque 500s; stacktraces never exposed — belt-and-braces `include-stacktrace: never`), AuthService (self-register → STUDENT only), UserView (no hash exposure), all learner routes `/me`-scoped (no tamperable ids), teacher/admin surfaces route-protected + regression-proven. No secret logging found.
- Empirical boundaries: 401/403 matrices, CORS allow+reject, content gate (only VALIDATED versions exist; serving rule regression-proven in ITs).
- **Hardening candidate (not fixed — small change, operator's call):** springdoc/OpenAPI endpoints are `permitAll` in prod; the schema is publicly reachable on a deployed Render URL. Candidate fix: disable springdoc in `application-prod.yml`. No credentials/PII in the schema (DTOs only), so low severity for the pilot.

### Performance (rehearsal, warm)

health 6 ms · subjects 12 ms · questions 10 ms · tutor refusal 11 ms · **NBA 62 ms** · **KG 76 ms**. Expect +RTT and Render free-tier cold start 30–60 s (documented in the runbook + surfaced honestly by the web client). No premature optimization warranted.

### Deliverables

- `download/t036/OPERATOR_DEPLOYMENT_PACKAGE.md` — accounts table, **safe-config vs secrets checklist**, exact Neon/Render/Vercel sequence, post-deploy verification checklist, pilot ops (teacher provisioning, data classification DEMO/TEST/PILOT, weekly rhythm, failure procedures table incl. LLM outage / Neon suspend / Flyway collision / CORS mismatch), content-pipeline notes.
- E2E screenshots: `download/t036/e2e-01…07` (dashboard fresh/wrong-answer result/NBA evolution/history/student KG tree+graph/tutor refusal/teacher surface).
- Scripts persisted: `scripts/t036_build_core.sh`, `t036_boot_core.sh`, `t036_verify_backend.py` (19-check operator checklist), `t036_open_pr.py`, `t036_open_web_pr.py`.

### Stage summary

- **Deployment is rehearsed end-to-end at production fidelity with two real bugs found, fixed, and regression-proven on CI-green PRs.** The remaining critical path is exactly one operator action set: merge PRs #12 + #7, then execute the operator package (Neon → Render → Vercel → optional LLM key), then re-run the runbook checklist against the deployed URLs.

## Session 24 — Render backend LIVE (deploy 4) + 12/12 live-API verification; docs updated for the Vercel half

**Date:** 2026-09-10 · **Mode:** deploy/verify (T-036)

**Directive:** user messages — deploy logs (3 attempts) + "This time it deployed successfully" + "Update all the docs now. TODO, progress etc. And also guide me through the vercel deployment."

Work performed:

- **Deploy 3 diagnosed** (commit `91441c0` — the Groq-with-key fix from deploy 2's finding): build OK, httpClient error GONE, but the app stalled silently ≥10 min after `HHH10001005` (mid-EntityManagerFactory) — no crash, no exit line — until Render's port scan timed out. Root cause: JVM footprint maxima ~850 MB (heap 75 % = 384 MB + UNBOUNDED metaspace + 240 MB code cache) against the 512 MB free-instance cgroup → kernel reclaim + full-GC thrash during SessionFactory construction (36 entities; Hibernate 7 + Spring AI 2 + Kotlin class loading); GC is CPU-bound on the 0.1-CPU quota, so each full GC takes minutes of wall time → zero progress, zero logs, process alive.
- **Fix `b35aa62`** (Dockerfile only): MaxRAMPercentage=42, MaxMetaspaceSize=160m, ReservedCodeCacheSize=48m, ActiveProcessorCount=1, UseSerialGC, TieredStopAtLevel=1 (C1-only — startup is CPU-bound on 0.1 CPU and C2 compilation was eating the quota), Xss512k, ExitOnOutOfMemoryError (future walls fail fast + visibly instead of stalling silently), EXPOSE 10000 (the real Render port). Pushed; core-ci green.
- **Deploy 4 verified from the operator's log:** `Started SyllabAiApplication in 135.598 seconds` (was: never), `Tomcat started on port 10000`, ALL THREE LLM providers registered (groq llama-3.3-70b-versatile / gemini-2.5-flash / openrouter — the operator configured all keys), R2-less boot WARN as designed, health check passed, deploy Live.
- **Post-deploy API verification 12/12 GREEN** (`scripts/t036_verify_deploy.py`, fresh throwaway user per run): health UP; register 201 + JWT → login 200 → me 200; 8 seeded MCQs (type `MCQ_SINGLE`) with no answer-key/misconception leakage in the DTO; wrong answer on Q1 distractor A → `correct=false`, `implicatedMisconceptionIds=[MIS-T1.1-01]` (the Paper-B evidence loop, live); correct answer → marks 1/1; attempt-history read model total=2; NBA 4 actions (ASK_TUTOR + 3×PRACTISE_QUESTIONS) off the weak-mastery signal; teacher 403 / admin 403 / anonymous 401. Two initial script-side assertion mismatches (type literal, wrapped history object) probed against the live JSON and corrected — app behavior was correct both times.
- **Tutor probe** `POST /api/v1/tutor/ask` → honest deterministic-refusal with guidance (no course material ingested — designed behavior; the LLM chain itself constructs cleanly with real keys, which was deploy 2's exact failure point).
- **CORS live-probed** (OPTIONS preflights against the deployed URL): allow-list = the application.yml DEFAULT (`http://localhost:3000`, `https://syllabai.vercel.app`); `syllabai-web.vercel.app` is rejected (403). Consequence: the Vercel project must be NAMED `syllabai` (URL `https://syllabai.vercel.app`) for zero-backend-change CORS, or the operator sets `SYLLABAI_CORS_ORIGINS` in Render.
- **Docs updated (this commit):** TODO.md T-036 session-24 status (backend half done + verified; remaining = Vercel import, teacher account, browser E2E); PROGRESS.md headline + Last-updated + 2 new findings (production-only failure classes; CORS default allow-list) + Next-work ACTIVE paragraph corrected; this WORKLOG entry. `download/t036/RENDER_FAILED_DEPLOY_TRIAGE.md` rewritten earlier this session (RESOLVED AND VERIFIED + 3-failure history + deploy-4 results table).

Stage summary:

- **T-036 backend half COMPLETE: the deployed environment is real and verified** — Neon (PostgreSQL 18.6 @ V14) + Render (`https://syllabai-core.onrender.com`, 4 deploys, 3 production-only bug classes fixed: base-image tag, with-key construction, cgroup memory) + the full LLM failover chain live.

## Session 25 — IGCSE-corpus pre-ingestion architecture (T-C05): CONTENT_CORPUS_ARCHITECTURE.md

**Date:** 2026-09-10 · **Mode:** architecture design (content-ops track, contract-only)

**Directive:** user message — continuing SyllabAI work with a new focus on the personally prepared academic corpus for **Pearson Edexcel International GCSE Chemistry** (explicitly NOT IAL; do not change Cycle 1). The operator manually converts (ocr.z.ai for past papers; planned manual Markdown for Student Book, specification, Save My Exams notes & exam questions). Requested: content architecture, metadata conventions, ingestion workflow, validation model, and repo organization so the manual Markdown imports cleanly later — across 14 specified analysis areas incl. an IAL↔IGCSE coexistence audit and a practical pre-ingestion standard.

Work performed:

- **Ground-truth load:** re-read the content/ingestion implementation before designing — V2 (curriculum + KG: `curriculum_versions`, `knowledge_nodes` with globally-unique `code` + validation_status, node types SUBJECT/UNIT/TOPIC/SUBTOPIC/MISCONCEPTION), V3 (questions/question_topics/options/attempts), V6 (IAL-CHEM-2018 seed: `CHM`/`WCH11-*` codes), V8 (exam_papers with `(paper_code, session_label)` identity, question_versions immutable snapshots, question_parts, mark_schemes, mark_points with `acceptance_criteria`), V11 (documents canonical store: checksum-idempotent, `kind` enum, pgvector chunks), V13 (GLM-OCR bridge records: verbatim QP/MS drafts, reconciliation OK/REVIEW_REQUIRED), plus the ingestion contracts (`GlmOcrPaperDraftDto` / `GlmOcrMarkSchemeDraftDto` — MS vocabulary allow/ignore/reject/ECF/alternatives/anyTwoFrom already structured; `CurriculumDraftDto` schema 1.1 with per-node provenance), `PastPaperIngestionService` (anchor-topic discipline, SUGGESTED-only), `CanonicalDocumentValidator`, and Master Spec §6.2/§6.4/§6.5/§7/§8/§10 (ADR-014, F-168, ADR-018). Also re-verified CORS live (preflight 200 + ACAO from `syllabai-web.vercel.app`; real POST echoes ACAO — still green after the earlier fix documented at `b64f564`).
- **Delivered `CONTENT_CORPUS_ARCHITECTURE.md` (T-C05, ~630 lines, contract-only — no code, no migration, no Cycle-1 change):** (1) five source roles (specification / student-book / past-paper / external-notes / external-questions) with a three-layer source model — shared SourceDocument spine (maps onto the V11 documents store), role-specific structural families (curriculum / instructional / assessment), unit-level validation; explicitly rejecting a flattened generic content model. (2) **Corpus Markdown Convention (CMC v1.0)** — the operator-facing contract: YAML front matter per role with a fixed key/value vocabulary, heading grammar (H1 doc / H2 question-chapter / H3 part-spec-point / H4 sub-part; numbering verbatim, never renumbered), the shared-stimulus rule (content between `## Question N` and the first `###` = shared context block — no special syntax), marks notation (`**(N marks)**` + `**(Total for Question N = M marks)**`), paper↔MS file pairing (`-ms.md` + `paper_doc`), MS table convention aligned to the parser's `(N)`-marker contract with Edexcel marking vocabulary preserved verbatim, chemistry notation rules (HTML sub/sup tags, `→`/`⇌` literals, ASCII minus, no unicode sub/sup — the anti-OCR-decay set), local `assets/` + `fig-<locator>-<seq>` naming + mandatory descriptive alt text + `<!-- figure-missing: -->` honesty markers, machine-flag HTML comments (`page:`, `qwc`, `either-or:`, `spec:` source-stated mappings only). (3) Canonical metadata contract mapping every front-matter field to its DB destination. (4) Chemistry-specific OCR lint (17 checks: hard errors = mark arithmetic, QP↔MS pairing, numbering continuity, schema, image refs, tables; warnings = unicode sub/sup, formula-adjacent digits, ASCII arrows, dash-minus, comma decimals, equilibrium heuristics, answer-key leakage, dedup fingerprints) with the human-review split (equations, numeric values, figure-adjacent questions, MS guidance, spec wording). (5) Validation lifecycle: pipeline stages RAW→…→SERVABLE × six orthogonal validation axes (parse / OCR fidelity / academic / mapping / marks-MS / release), each with validator kind and append-only records — a refinement of the existing SUGGESTED→VALIDATED machinery + bridge REVIEW_REQUIRED pattern, never a replacement; role-specific SERVABLE gates. (6) Spec-point mapping: four provenance tiers (PUBLISHER/PROVIDER · AI_SUGGESTED · RULE_DERIVED · HUMAN_VALIDATED), many-to-many default, hard rule against cross-curriculum mappings. (7) Question taxonomy: source facts vs canonical vs derived vs suggested→validated tags. (8) Difficulty: UNEVIDENCED→PROVISIONAL→EMERGING→EVIDENCED states with per-CurriculumVersion populations; the existing INT difficulty column reinterpreted as provisional prior (no migration forced). (9) Provenance/versioning chain (SourceDocument → canonical_json → units → SourceLocator → validation trail; corrections = new versions, lineage preserved). (10) Five-level dedup semantics (same source object / duplicate representation / similar / variant / related) — machine detection, human classification, never auto-delete. (11) `syllabai-corpus` repo layout keyed board/qualification-family/subject/curriculum-code with path-is-convenience + front-matter-is-truth. (12) System gap audit — verdicts per subsystem. (13) **IAL↔IGCSE coexistence audit** with six contamination vectors and guards. (14) Pre-Ingestion Standard: MUST (9) / SHOULD (6) / CAN EVOLVE (4) / DO NOT OVER-ENGINEER (5).
- **Audit findings registered as tasks:** T-C06 (parked) converter + `SPEC_POINT` node type + namespaced `4CH1-*` codes + F-168 mapping-provenance columns + `documents.kind`/`questions.provenance` enum extensions; **T-C07 curriculum-scoped retrieval** — `ContentRetrievalService.search(query, kind, limit)` + kind-agnostic `ContentVectorRetriever` carry NO curriculum dimension; latent only because the GLM-OCR corpus is quarantined and unembedded; mandatory before corpus embeddings are enabled; T-C08 (future) difficulty-evidence jobs. Honest positive finding: the assessment family + validation machinery are already corpus-grade (V8 + T-C02/C03, tested with real 4CH0/WPH11 fixtures), so the corpus needs an adapter, not a new pipeline.
- **Docs updated (this commit):** TODO.md (T-C05 done + T-C06/07/08 registered in the content-ops track), PROGRESS.md (Last-updated + Session-25 headline), this WORKLOG entry. No code repos touched; core/web mains unchanged (`b35aa62` / `376f4c5`).

Stage summary:

- **T-C05 complete: the manual 4CH1 corpus has a binding import contract before mass conversion begins** — the operator now standardizes front matter, headings, marks notation, chemistry notation, and asset conventions from file #1 (the MUST list), with everything else designed to evolve after the corpus exists.

## Session 28 — T-C09 EXECUTED: Phase-1 knowledge-graph skeleton extracted, PDF-verified, validated (syllabai-resources)

Continuation after a session interruption (worklog recoverable from the repo state): the
extraction script had been written but never run. This session ran it, fixed what the hard
gates caught, shipped the validator, and committed.

**First gated run failed exactly as gates should:** 2 PDF cross-check mismatches (1.60C,
2.50) — root cause: the PDF text layer's section-start sub-topic TOC listing
("The following sub-topics are covered in this section[:.]?" + title lines) glues onto the
previous section's last statement as "continuation text", because the section title is
extracted as two silently-skipped lines ('2' + 'Inorganic chemistry'). The S3 variant of the
prefix ends with ':' (not '.'), so it slipped the skip regex too; 3.22C was silently polluted
into a false fuzzy pass. Fix: listing-state machine in `pdf_boundary_map()` (prefix line
enters the state; listing titles are skipped, not glued; state clears at the next real
subsection header / code line / section title / 'Students should:'). Second defect: the
45-table classifier checked `paper_overview` ("Paper code" in flat text) BEFORE
`codes_appendix` ("Subject codes") — Appendix 1's "Paper codes" row substring-matched and
emitted a bogus third paper record with null marks/duration. Fix: check codes_appendix first
+ a `papers: 2` gate + null-marks gate.

**Final gated run: ALL GREEN.** 182 spec points (S1:60/S2:50/S3:22/S4:50) · 52 C-points ·
4 topics · 28 subtopics · 12 practicals · 25 command words · 45 tables (31 spec) · 210
PART_OF edges · 2 papers. Ground truth amended vs the plan §5 baseline: 182/52/28 (not
167/40/29) — the md holds all 182 statements but 4.49C/4.50C are OCR-orphans outside the
HTML tables; documented in `graph/reports/PHASE1_COMPLETENESS.md` §2. Statement-level PDF
cross-check: 119 exact / 52 normalized / 11 fuzzy (residual OCR damage) / **0 mismatch**;
statement order identical; md↔PDF code sets equal. Damage policy honored: text verbatim
(whitespace-normalised only), 54/182 statements carry damage flags, never silently fixed;
4SD0 whitelisted only inside the Double-Award applicability rule prose.

**Deliverables (all in syllabai-resources, private repo, zero core changes):**
- `scripts/c09_spec_graph_extract.py` — deterministic zero-LLM extractor, hard gates abort
  on any count/mismatch regression; regenerates the whole `graph/` tree.
- `graph/` = 6 YAML graph-as-code files per KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_CONTEXT §8A.14:
  `specification_points.yaml` (182 records: verbatim wording, subsection, orderings,
  C-point applicability, leading verb → draft SKILL tags, full provenance incl. md line
  anchor + table/row index + row shape + PDF page + match level, damage flags),
  `topics.yaml` (4 + 28 with md/PDF title reconciliation and header source),
  `relationships.yaml` (210 PART_OF edges, live V2 enum names only),
  `command_words.yaml` (25: 23 main + 2 categorised), `practicals.yaml` (12),
  `assessment_objectives.yaml` (3 AOs + 2 papers).
- `graph/reports/PHASE1_COMPLETENESS.md` (12 sections: gates table, baseline amendment

  45-table census, subsection inventory, practicals, command words, C-point rule,
  skill-tag census incl. 9 unknown-leading-verb entries, operator actions)
  + `graph/reports/SPOT_CHECK_SHEET.md` (seeded 20-statement sample, reproducible,
  PASS/FAIL checkboxes).
- `scripts/graph_check.py` — the T-C09 validator: 8 check groups (meta/count consistency;

## Session 29 — T-C10 EXECUTED: Phase-2 revision-note mapping built, gated, negative-tested (syllabai-resources)

Executed on the operator's explicit "Proceed with T-C10" instruction (the TODO's
"after operator review" condition for Phase 1 is thereby superseded by the operator's
own sequencing decision; the T-C09 spot-check/PR gates remain open for the operator).

**Method (per KNOWLEDGE_GRAPH_BUILD_PLAN §6 + CONTEXT §8A.7, slug signal above semantic
similarity, uncertainty preserved):**
1. `scripts/c10_worksheets.py` (deterministic, zero-LLM): parsed all 112 notes' source
   URLs, HARD-verified the 28 SME topic-group slugs align 1:1 (count AND ordering) with
   the 28 spec subsections, then emitted 28 per-subsection worksheets (spec statements +
   note headings/excerpts) + a full 182-point registry appendix for the AI pass.
2. AI mapping pass (GLM, Super Z agent): read every worksheet, wrote 211 point-level
   mapping decisions to `scripts/c10_decisions/S{1..4}.json` — each with code,
   confidence (high/medium/low), verbatim evidence quote, and rationale.
3. `scripts/c10_map_notes.py` (gated applier): injected a `spec_map:` front-matter block
   into each note — PROVIDER subsection anchor (tier/signal/slug, recomputed from the
   URL) + AI_SUGGESTED spec_points with per-mapping provenance (confidence,
   model_version, evidence, rationale, validation_status SUGGESTED).

**Hard gates (all green; the evidence gate caught 2 real quote defects during the build —
wrong-case/paraphrased quotes fixed to verbatim):**
- G3 anti-hallucination: every evidence quote verified to appear in the note after
  normalisation (markdown emphasis/HTML sub-sup tags/links/whitespace/curly variants).

## Session 55 — T-C11's settled graph wired into the learner Next-Best-Action loop (2026-09-13)

**Commission:** the operator's session-55 directive — "Use the settled T-C11 concept/prerequisite/misconception graph as an input to the existing learner-facing Next Best Action / recommendation path. The purpose is to turn the graph work into observable product behavior." Constraints honored end-to-end: no T-C11 reopen, no new ontology audit, no KG rebuild, no Neo4j/second graph DB, no LLM requirement, smallest-integration extension of the existing engine, output still the Next Best Learning Action abstraction, only HUMAN_VALIDATED relationships authoritative (the 3 pilot HOLDs + 2 REVIEW_REQUIRED edges excluded), graph never treated as learner state, deterministic tests for Cases A–D.

- **Architecture traced first** (per the directive): learner state (SkillState BKT / MisconceptionState BDT / ReviewSchedule, Postgres) → mastery/misconception evidence (AssessmentService events → LearnerModelService) → NBA (`NextBestActionService.actionsFor`, 7 deterministic tiers, ADR-017) → resource/question selection (`ServableQuestionService`) → learner-facing presentation (GET /api/v1/learners/me/recommendations → `NextBestActionsCard.tsx`). Integration point identified: the NBA's per-request subtree registry + evidence maps, where the KG (V6 seed) and learner evidence already meet — the T-C11 graph joins **by KG node code** (the stable identity both stores guarantee).
- **New dependency layer** `ConceptDependencyGraph` (+ `ConceptDependencyGraphLoader` + `ConceptGraphConfig` bean): the settled store's semantic edges, **HUMAN_VALIDATED only by construction** (the factory is the single filtering point — SUGGESTED/REVIEW_REQUIRED cannot enter), PART_OF dropped (curriculum structure stays the runtime KG's authority), endpoints integrity-checked fail-closed. The graph ships as **byte-verbatim classpath snapshots** of `graph/concept_edges.yaml` + `graph/concepts.yaml` + `graph/practicals.yaml` (cmp-verified against the resources repo), **SHA-256-pinned** — drift fails startup loudly; a snapshot upgrade is a conscious reviewed change, so T-C11 state can never silently change recommendation inputs.
- **Two graph-aware NBA stages (nba-rules/v1.1**, tiers and thresholds unchanged): **T2b** validated prerequisite chain (dependent established-weak from measured evidence + HUMAN_VALIDATED `REQUIRES_PREREQUISITE` edge → REVIEW_PREREQUISITE on the prerequisite, `VALIDATED_PREREQUISITE_CHAIN`; a prerequisite measured strong is skipped in favour of the evidence; unmeasured honestly reported as unmeasured) and **T4b** validated misconception remediation (active BDT state + HUMAN_VALIDATED `REMEDIATED_BY` edge → new `REMEDIATE_MISCONCEPTION` action on the corrective concept, `MISCONCEPTION_REMEDIATION`; the existing ASK_TUTOR on the misconception node is preserved). Both stages respect subject-subtree isolation, one-action-per-topic, and the maxActions cap; with no applicable validated relationship the existing behavior is byte-identical (Case C).
- **Frontend**: `NextBestActionType`/`NextBestReasonCode` unions extended; `NextBestActionsCard` renders REMEDIATE_MISCONCEPTION ("Fix misconception", teal chip, BookOpenCheck icon) with "Practise" when validated questions exist on the corrective concept, else the Ask-Tutor button (the Cycle-1 grounded remediation surface). `tsc --noEmit` clean.
- **Tests (304/304 green, +27 new)**: `ConceptDependencyGraphTest` (factory filter, fail-closed relation/endpoint/duplicate, ordering), `ConceptDependencyGraphLoaderTest` (the real packaged snapshot: 153 validated / 112 RP / 14 RB / 13 WAP / 9 EB / 2 CCW / 2 MO / 1 RL; the frozen five provably absent; determinism; tamper tests through the parse seam — one demoted status ⇒ 152, one dangling endpoint ⇒ fail), `NextBestActionServiceTest` graph group (Case A with the REAL BEC→COVALENT-BOND chain incl. evidence-gate + no-evidence + subject-isolation negatives; Case B with the REAL MIS-BOND-ENERGY-COUNT→BEC remediation incl. threshold negative; Case C baseline-identity comparison; Case D with the REAL frozen five; learner isolation; determinism; maxActions cap; and a full test through the production loader — the actual 153-edge settled store driving both stages), plus `ConceptGraphRemediationFlowIT` (real Postgres + real KG rows carrying settled codes + real state rows + the production graph bean; CI/Docker-gated, skips cleanly in this Docker-less environment). Baseline before any edit: 277/277 green; all 14 pre-existing NBA tests pass with `ConceptDependencyGraph.empty()` — the v1 baseline regression proof.
- **Docs**: dated session-55 amendment in `RECOMMENDATION_SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md` §6 (validated graph relationships as candidate sources 10/11, hard boundaries recorded); `application.yml` recommendation block comment notes v1.1 (no new config — the layer is code-side).
- **Commits**: syllabai-core (layer + stages + snapshot + tests), syllabai-web (types + card), tracker (this entry + PROGRESS + TODO). All pushed to origin/main.
- **Environment note**: local toolchain (JDK 25 + Maven 3.9.16) provisioned this session to run the suite; Docker unavailable here so the IT executes in CI (its compilation + registration verified via `mvn verify`).
- **State**: T-C11 graph → observable product behavior: the learner loop demonstrably produces graph-supported Next Best Learning Actions from real learner evidence, with deterministic tests pinning every boundary of the directive. No blocking defects. NEXT (per the directive's continuation): the remaining Phase-2 roadmap — teacher-side KG work and pilot readiness are unblocked; a future 4CH1 KG seed (teacher-side content path) will activate the graph stages against real curriculum rows automatically via the code join.

Student paths: A (ordinary learning) verified on PRODUCTION (WCH11 MCQ loop, deterministic marking, state, NBA, history, retry — mastery 0.113→0.383, misconception 0.75→0.5); B/C (misconception + prerequisite remediation through the VALIDATED graph) verified over real HTTP on the seeded 4CH1 graph with real BKT/BDT evidence rows: REVIEW_PREREQUISITE/VALIDATED_PREREQUISITE_CHAIN (BEC→BOND-BREAKING-MAKING, BEC→COVALENT-BOND) at ranks 1–2 + ASK_TUTOR preserved at rank 3 + REMEDIATE_MISCONCEPTION/MISCONCEPTION_REMEDIATION on the corrective concept at rank 4 (the graph nominates, learner evidence gates, existing behavior preserved); D (no applicable relationship) verified — CON-ATOM weak → byte-identical baseline LOW_MASTERY/PRACTISE_QUESTIONS; E (retry changes state + recommendations deterministically) verified on production. The v1.1 card renders in the browser ("Fix misconception", "Review prerequisite", honest "none yet" servable counts).
- **Section 5 — isolation/security:** student A/B see only their own state (me-scoped, verified with two accounts); teacher routes 403 for students / 401 anonymous (prod + local); the teacher edge read model is byte-stable across learner evidence (learner state never leaks into teacher KG reads); V6 IAL tree contains ZERO 4CH1 codes and the 4CH1 edges view contains ZERO non-4CH1 endpoints (cross-subject isolation both directions); the frozen five (3 HOLD + 2 REVIEW_REQUIRED) provably absent from the seeded DB (all five identities checked, zero non-VALIDATED 4CH1 semantic edges). Known limitation recorded (not a pilot defect): all TEACHER-role holders share the same cohort-wide surfaces — acceptable for a single-teacher pilot, per-teacher partitioning is ADR-015 Cycle 2+.
- **Section 6 — data integrity:** SpecificationPoints remain the authoritative curriculum anchors (concepts hang under them, PART_OF, SUGGESTED); the 153 HUMAN_VALIDATED edges land VALIDATED with full T-C11 provenance (112 RP + 14 RB + 13 WAP + 9 EB + 2 CCW + 2 MO + 1 RL, verified in the live DB); no duplicate canonical concepts (unique code + resolve-by-code reuse: re-run reuses 340/496, zero new rows); attempts are append-only evidence (no update path exists).
- **Three shipped defects found + fixed (the genuine blockers):**
  1. **core 5991187 — the teacher concept-graph edge read model served 124 of 153 edges** (all 29 misconception-family edges dropped: REMEDIATED_BY 14 / WAP 13 / MO 2 never appeared for teachers). Root cause: the read model scopes to the PART_OF subtree, but misconceptions attach via family edges (they are edge sources, not PART_OF members). Fix: `findMisconceptionFamilyEdgesWithin` + the controller widens the endpoint scope with exactly those misconceptions (the same widening the tree fold applies). This was the assertion the CI-red `ConceptGraphSeedFlowIT` was pinning. Also fixed the activation summary's off-by-one (root node not counted: 339 reported vs 340 real — the UI summary line inherited it). Also `MissingServletRequestParameterException` → honest 400 (was generic 500, reproduced on production).
  2. **core f7adea7 — subject-scoped practice:** `GET /api/v1/questions?rootId=` scopes the servable list to a subject's subtree (same servability boundary, topicNodeId keeps priority, unscoped unchanged). The unscoped list had served the WCH11 demo MCQs under the 4CH1 workbench (landing evidence the learner's subject-scoped panels could not see). New IT `practiceIsSubjectScoped` pins 4CH1→0 / CHM→8 against the real seeds.
  3. **web ca01d9e — subject selector + honest practice empty state:** AppHeader gains a subject picker (renders only when >1 subject exists; switching re-scopes graph/recommendations/state/practice) and PracticeView requests the subject-scoped list with a subject-aware empty state ("No validated questions are linked to <subject> yet"). Verified in the browser: 4CH1 → honest empty practice; switch to Chemistry (IAL) → the 8 MCQs serve; dashboard re-scopes; zero console errors.
- **Verification state after the fixes:** local unit suite 334/334 green; **CI green on both repos** (core-ci: 334 unit + 33 IT incl. the previously-red ConceptGraphSeedFlowIT now passing with all its 340/496/153/isolation/idempotency assertions; web-ci: lint + production build). CI had been RED on core main since session-56's 2c699a3 (the IT never ran locally — Testcontainers needs Docker; the "329/329 green" claim was the unit suite only). Live re-verification on a fresh DB: fresh activation 340/496/153, re-run 0/340+496, edges view exactly the settled composition, frozen five absent, V6 untouched.
- **Pilot readiness verdict: READY WITH CONDITIONS** (no architecture/review phase opened; the blockers were all small shipped-code or operational gaps, now fixed or specified): the student learning loop is production-operational TODAY on the WCH11 demo substrate (8 MCQs — the pilot's honest content ceiling); the teacher 4CH1 concept-graph surface and the graph-aware learner NBA are functionally complete and CI-proven but need four operator actions on production: (1) Vercel redeploy of web main (the deployed bundle predates session-55/56 — without it the teacher concept-graph UI and the v1.1 card do not exist in production), (2) teacher account provisioning per DEPLOYMENT.md §2, (3) the one-click 4CH1 activation (teacher UI or POST), (4) accept the documented cold-start (~1–3 min) and weekly evidence export. No unrelated changes.
- **Validation contract read from the implementation (Phase 3):** the authoritative transition is `SUGGESTED → (staged hash-chained decision log: VALIDATE/REJECT/FLAG/REVERSE) → r3-5 gated importer → VALIDATED/REJECTED → ServableQuestionSpec → learner-servable`. There is no intermediate state between SUGGESTED and VALIDATED — FLAG is an events-only annotation (state stays SUGGESTED, recorded in `teacher_validation_events` with result_state SUGGESTED), REVERSE restores the pre-decision state and must reference the last applied decision. Serving gate (V18 + ServableQuestionSpec/ServableQuestionService): MCQ serves when active (the seed rule); STRUCTURED requires the current version VALIDATED — the campaign is 100% STRUCTURED, so the validation boundary is exactly on `question_versions.validation_state`. Subject-surface serving additionally requires KG topic mapping (PART_OF subtree) — campaign content is under the GEN ingestion subject with no mapping, so validation alone never surfaces content under CHM/WCH11 (isolation by construction; T-C06/F-168 mapping is the separate parked step for 4CH1 surfaces).
- **Negative/dry-run tests FIRST (Phase 7 directive): 7/7 fail-closed gates fired, 0 DB rows written** (importer `check` mode = full gate stack read-only): unknown question-version target → "nonexistent or quarantined content"; seed-VALIDATED target (the WCH11 lock) → "seed lock / lifecycle violation"; malformed log line → strict-reader abort; tampered entry (note mutated after signing) → "hash mismatch (entry tampered)"; broken prevHash link → "link break"; seq discontinuity → refused; REVERSE-of-never-applied → "state moved on since; refusing". ABORT manifests archived under `download/teacher-validation/runs/`. Two hardening observations recorded (not fixed — out of scope, no safety impact): malformed logs fail via uncaught exception rather than a graceful manifest (still fail-closed, nothing written); non-whitelisted action values (e.g. a hypothetical APPROVE) are silently skipped by the planner (no UPDATE can be generated for them; V18 CHECK + the workbench writer constrain actions to the four legal values).

Recovery (same session): Neon control-plane API reached via `console.neon.tech/api/v2` (`api.neon.tech` has no public A record; org/project/branch discovered; `neondb_owner` password RESET); complete 9-var env set restored in ONE atomic PUT (DB coordinates w/ new password, fresh generated JWT, CORS, storage, profiles, campaign label/commit); deploy **LIVE**; health/CORS/register/subjects verified; prod data intact (users, seeds, canary rows all present). **The one non-restorable item: the 3 LLM provider keys + embedding key** (values existed only in the wiped config; the running process was not readable; the old password was rotated so only the new one exists). Until the operator re-provisions them, the tutor endpoint returns its honest 503 ("set SYLLABAI_GROQ_API_KEY") — the designed degradation, all other functionality verified green.

**Positive side-effect:** the restored config now carries `SYLLABAI_CAMPAIGN_LABEL/COMMIT`, so the production app's own boots MAINTAIN the campaign DB identity claim (label stays `T-C04-CAMPAIGN` across spin-down/wake — the race that aborted the first 16-batch launch is structurally closed).

## Session 71 — Productization Sprint 2 §8–§10 EXECUTED: the adaptive teacher–learner loop closed end-to-end (Smart Lesson v2 + structured Tutor signals V25 + class-weakness Test Builder targeting; backend verified live at f0923a1; monitor green on all three new checks) (2026-09-15)

**Sprint-2 continuation (directive: close the adaptive teacher–learner loop, autonomous).** Sessions 69/70 closed §1–§7. This session delivered the §8–§10 tranche and the §11–§13 verifications — the final product loop of the sprint: VALIDATED CONTENT → STUDENT → ATTEMPT/TUTOR → LEARNING EVIDENCE → LEARNER MODEL → SMART LESSON/NBA → MARKING → CLASS EVIDENCE → TEACHER DIAGNOSIS → TARGETED TEST → STUDENT.

**§8 Smart Lesson v2 (smart-lesson/v2) — IMPLEMENTED / VERIFIED:**
- Same ladder shape, same thresholds, no new learner state, no ML, no second engine. The advance/consolidation behavior is now genuinely evidence-aware: most-recent confusion-family signal first → most-overdue due review anywhere in the subject → prerequisite-READY unstarted topics (measured-weak blockers defer; every-unstarted-blocked ⇒ REMEDIATE the weakest blocker) → standing weak/unstarted rules; consolidation targets the STALEST measured topic (evidence freshness); misconception ties break to the freshest BDT evidence; review details name the due date and overdue-ness.
- Repeated-exposure avoidance: the deterministic starter question is the first servable question the learner has NOT attempted (one batched attempted-ids query per response); all-attempted revisits the first, honestly.
- §8 closed-loop proof (SmartLessonFlowIT, real Postgres + real events, not fixture swaps): cold INSUFFICIENT_COVERAGE → misconception-tagged wrong answer → MISCONCEPTION_SUSPECTED (reason = the new BDT evidence); mastered → advance to T1.2 → REAL TutorAnsweredEvent doubt signal → advance target redirects to T2.1; attempted starter → next practice hands the unattempted question ("1 of 4").
- One fetch per evidence source per response (reviews, prerequisite relations, windowed engagements hoisted — the v1 ladder re-queried per rung); §11 posture preserved.

**§9 structured Tutor signals (tutor-signals/v2, V25) — IMPLEMENTED / VERIFIED:**
- Two new RECORD-TIME signal types, both deterministic (never LLM-derived): PREREQUISITE_HELP (the tutor policy's rule-output plan chose PREREQUISITE_REVIEW) and CLARIFICATION_REQUEST (fixed phrase list, checked after doubt phrases, before explanation patterns — "what do you mean" is a clarification, not an explanation request).
- V25 migration: expanded CHECK + classifier_version provenance column (legacy rows stay 'tutor-signals/v1'); every row keeps learner, topic anchor, occurred_at, grounding strength, refused flag, model identity, surface/context — raw chat text still never reaches learner state, and no LLM writes any learner-state field.
- Multi-row signals (repeated explanation request ≥2, unresolved question = the refused flag, post-explanation engagement strictly-later) are DERIVED at read time by TutorSignalPolicy — stated as evidence in Smart Lesson rung 6, never mastery.
- Consumed by: Smart Lesson v2 (confusion family + derived-signal evidence), NBA v1.3 (T7a detail carries the per-topic signal mix; ordering unchanged; reader default-method keeps test fakes honest), teacher analytics (tutorSignalCounts absorbs the new types automatically).

**§10 class-weakness Test Builder targeting (test-builder-weakness/v1) — IMPLEMENTED / VERIFIED:**
- GET /api/v1/teacher/tests/weakness-options: weak class areas with EXPLICIT reasons only (LOW_MEAN_MASTERY / ACTIVE_MISCONCEPTION_PRESENT / BLOCKED_BY_WEAK_PREREQUISITE) + the raw aggregates behind them — no synthetic weakness score. Unmeasured topics NEVER claimed weak; listed separately as honest coverage gaps.
- servableQuestions on every option is the TARGETING count (primary + secondary mappings — the exact /preview assembly rule; two batched queries, no per-topic N+1; the primary-keyed analytics count would understate what a test can draw from the topic).
- Ordering: mean mastery asc (nulls last) → misconception learners desc → code. Assembly through the unchanged /preview: VALIDATED-only, marks-targeted, deterministic, no duplicates, honest undershoot.
- Web (d4edad6): Test Builder "Target class weaknesses" panel (reason badges, aggregates, select-all, collapsible coverage-gap lane); tsc + production build clean.

**§11–§13 verifications:** §11 — the Teacher→Class→Topic→affected learners→evidence→intervention chain is complete and the last hop is now code, not convention (WeaknessTargetingFlowIT drives class evidence → weakness option → targeted preview → answer key end-to-end over real HTTP with RBAC 401/403/200, determinism, leaf-scope isolation and the unknown-topic negative). §12 — the marking→learner-state path pinned: a PENDING structured answer is INVISIBLE to the learner model (honest gap preserved); the first authoritative human mark fires the evidence and the Smart Lesson action changes for that reason; the κ gate was not touched. §13 — the minimal intervention loop is the §10 flow (evidence → targeted test → answer key); students-attempt→marking→class-evidence-changes is the existing §6 lane.

**Production verification (live, PILOT_TEACHER_* pattern, pilot-monitor run 34962817917):** learner-nba → **nba-rules/v1.3** OK; **smart-lesson → smart-lesson/v2, action INSUFFICIENT_COVERAGE** OK (honest cold start on the fresh probe learner); **weakness-targeting → 200, 0 weak / 7 coverage gaps** OK (0 weak is the honest state: 0/325 topics measured because 249 structured answers are still PENDING — the weakness lane lights up as the teacher marks; the 7 gaps prove the unmeasured-with-activity lane works); marking-lane OK (249 pending / 15 groups, chain OK); class-analytics OK (enrolled=67); contamination 0; tutor shape OK. Only RED: the KNOWN web-bundle stale drift (session-69 Vercel git-author operator blocker — the frontend bundle still serves the 96d7a34-era code until the operator connects nawaf-al-hussain to the Vercel team or re-provides the vcp_ token; every API surface including the three new ones is live and monitor-verified regardless).

SMART LESSON RESPONSE (run 34971418531 @ 12:52): the learner's action changed for the right reason — `INSUFFICIENT_COVERAGE` ("No attempt evidence on 4CH1-S2-f yet") → **`LOW_MASTERY` ("Measured mastery 0.12 over 3 attempts is below the weak ceiling (0.45) — keep practising 4CH1-S2-f")**, evidence trace carrying attempts=3 + raw/decay-adjusted mastery, topic status ESTABLISHED; class view after: S2-f 8 measured learners, mean 0.1137, 14 evidence-backed attempts; 10 weak topics total.**
- **Real defect found BY the cycle (this is why the cycle was worth running as production work):** multi-part structured attempts fired their evidence event at the FIRST part's authoritative mark with the PARTIAL attempt total — the monitor's two 6/6 attempts fired as INCORRECT (4/6 and 1/6 at the instant of their first parts' marks), so full-mark remediation never credited mastery (measured 0.12 where ~0.31 was earned), and the settled attempt row (`correct=true`) disagreed with the evidence event (`correct=false`) — exactly what `Attempt.recordTotalMarks`' documented contract forbids. **Fix (core `fe01b87`, rebased clean over the concurrent CLA lane's `6092650`):** the evidence event waits until the attempt has no PENDING answer rows, then fires ONCE with the settled full total — same once-only guard, same fail-closed stance; single-part attempts unchanged; the κ-released Smart Mark path gets the same completion rule. New multi-part regression test (part-a mark → no evidence + partial row sum; completing mark → one event, full 4-mark total, correctness true). Local unit suite **495 green** (1 pre-existing skip). Already-fired events are immutable by design; the three mis-observed events remain as historical data, documented.
- **Deliberately NOT done:** κ/evaluate — the sample is placeholder-degenerate (all-zero human awards; κ would be a manufactured 1.0 and would RELEASE Smart Mark authority on evidence that never tested it). κ waits for the pilot's real mixed-award marking. Also left for its lane: the 10 new PENDING answers from the concurrent CLA eval bundle (created 12:43–12:47, untouched per the concurrent-lane protocol).

Render auto-deploy is webhook-driven and presumed applied (Dockerfile builds -DskipTests; health UP); not independently verifiable without the Render token.
